- **[Vocal Image](https://topxai.cn/ai/vocal-image)** — AI 声音与表达教练，录音后即时评估音高、清晰度与语速，并生成每日训练计划，适合日常练口音、演讲与面试等真实表达的场景。 · [website](https://vocalimage.app/)
- **[Vocalize](https://topxai.cn/ai/vocalize)** — AI 翻唱与文字转语音站点，可从社区声音库中选择音色生成歌曲翻唱或配音，官网称已累计制作数百万条 AI 翻唱并提供数万种声音。 · [website](https://www.vocalize.fm/)
- **[Vocol AI](https://topxai.cn/ai/vocol-ai)** — 面向亚洲团队的多语种语音协作平台，可把会议、访谈、播客录音转写为文字并提炼重点，自动抽取行动项，官方称支持中文、日文与英文。 · [website](https://www.vocol.ai/)
- **[Voice Changer](https://topxai.cn/ai/voice-changer)** — Voice Changer 是一个免费的在线变声器，上传音频、用麦克风录音或输入文字生成语音，即可套用怪物、机器人、电话、洞穴回声等数十种效果，适合恶搞配音、短视频与音效试验。 · [website](https://voicechanger.io/)
- **[VoiceDub](https://topxai.cn/ai/voicedub)** — 浏览器内的 AI 翻唱工作室，可用上万种社区声音替换人声制作翻唱，也提供声音克隆、二重唱、人声分离与文字转语音等工具，支持导出 WAV 与分轨。 · [website](https://voicedub.ai/)
- **[Voicemaker](https://topxai.cn/ai/voicemaker)** — Online text-to-speech tool with 2000+ AI voices across 130 languages and MP3 or WAV export, aimed at YouTube and short video, plus voice cloning and speech-to-text. · [website](https://voicemaker.in/)
- **[Voicemod](https://topxai.cn/ai/voicemod)** — Windows 与 Mac 上的实时变声器兼音效板，内置 200 多个 AI 音色与社区上传的声音，可在语音聊天、游戏语音与直播中即时切换，官网称低延迟并带降噪与人声增强。 · [website](https://www.voicemod.net)
- **[Voicv](https://topxai.cn/ai/voicv)** — Voicv 是语音克隆与合成平台，提供零样本声音克隆、文字转语音与语音转文字，支持多语言、情感控制与实时处理，并含 AI 数字人与 API 接入。 · [website](https://voicv.com/)
- **[Voiser](https://topxai.cn/ai/voiser)** — 土耳其团队做的语音平台，一边把文字转成多语言配音，一边把音视频转成文稿，还提供声音克隆、数字人与网站朗读，适合视频出海、播客转写与多语种本地化。 · [website](https://voiser.net/)
- **[Voiser AI](https://topxai.cn/ai/voiser-ai)** — Voiser AI 是集文字转语音、语音转文字、AI 视频与声音克隆于一体的多语言平台，官方称支持 140 多种语言，提供带情绪的声音与发音自定义。 · [website](https://voiser.ai/)
- **[Wavel AI](https://topxai.cn/ai/wavel-ai)** — AI 视频生成与配音平台，支持脚本转视频、声音克隆、多语言配音与自动字幕，并提供口型同步、短视频切片与画质增强等编辑能力。 · [website](https://wavel.ai/)
- **[WellSaid](https://topxai.cn/ai/wellsaid)** — Enterprise AI voice platform promising the most realistic AI voices, with voiceover timbres across dialects and production styles, built with security and compliance in mind. · [website](https://www.wellsaidlabs.com/)
- **[WhisperTranscribe](https://topxai.cn/ai/whispertranscribe)** — WhisperTranscribe 基于 OpenAI Whisper 的音频转录工具，提供 95% 准确率与说话人识别，支持 55+ 语言、与音频对话及自动剪辑。 · [website](https://www.whispertranscribe.com/)
- **[Willow Voice](https://topxai.cn/ai/willow-voice)** — Willow 是一款跨应用的 AI 语音听写工具，按住快捷键说话，排版好的文字直接落进光标处，支持 100 多种语言与离线使用。适合写邮件、回消息和给 AI 写提示词的人。 · [website](https://willowvoice.com/)
- **[Wispr Flow](https://topxai.cn/ai/wispr-flow)** — Wispr Flow 是语音转文字工具，说出来的话会在你说话时被清理成通顺文字，自动识别语种、学习你的专有名词并按不同应用切换语气，现已含会议记录功能。 · [website](https://wisprflow.ai/)
- **[Wusheng AI](https://topxai.cn/ai/wusheng-ai)** — Hyper-realistic AI speech synthesis and instant voice cloning (formerly Reecho), with in-house models covering 30+ languages and dialects, cloning from as little as three seconds of audio, plus a voice marketplace and low-latency API. · [website](https://www.wusound.cn/)
- **[音剪](https://topxai.cn/ai/ximalaya-audioeditor)** — 喜马拉雅旗下的一站式在线音频创作平台，官方描述为多情感多模态的 AI 辅助工具，提供在线剪辑能力，用于播客与音频作品创作。 · [website](https://audioeditor.ximalaya.com/)
- **[iFlytek Zhizuo](https://topxai.cn/ai/xunfei-zhizuo)** — iFlytek's one-stop AI dubbing and digital human platform, offering near-human text-to-speech, cartoon digital human video and avatars, and custom voices for training, media and marketing. · [website](https://peiyin.xunfei.cn/)
- **[云幕同声](https://topxai.cn/ai/yunmu-tongsheng)** — 原声级 AI 视频翻译工具，用声音复刻模型实现高保真多语种翻译，覆盖短剧出海、电商带货与课程教学，保留音色与背景氛围，助力内容全球化分发。 · [website](https://www.yunmuts.com/)

## Image Recognition & Analysis

<sub>[↑ Back to top](#table-of-contents) · `31 tools`</sub>

- **[ArcSoft Vision](https://topxai.cn/ai/arcsoft-ai)** — ArcSoft's vision AI platform, centred on a free face recognition SDK with detection, comparison, search, liveness detection and heavy-occlusion recognition, deployable on device or server for access control and attendance. · [website](https://ai.arcsoft.com.cn/)
- **[Baidu AI Cloud Image Recognition](https://topxai.cn/ai/baidu-image-recognition)** — Baidu AI Cloud's image recognition API, recognising 100,000 object and scene types with dedicated models for animals, plants, flowers, dishes, logos and vehicle damage assessment. · [website](https://cloud.baidu.com/product/imagerecognition)
- **[BigJPG](https://topxai.cn/ai/bigjpg)** — AI image upscaling with deep convolutional neural networks: up to 16x with smart denoising, separately tuned for illustrations and photos, rebuilding lines and detail rather than stretching pixels. Desktop app, API and old photo restoration. · [website](https://bigjpg.com/)
- **[Clipdrop](https://topxai.cn/ai/clipdrop)** — AI image editing toolkit that cuts out, removes objects, upscales, outpaints, swaps backgrounds and erases text in seconds, with platform-ready sizes and an API for developers. · [website](https://clipdrop.co/)
- **[Cutout.pro](https://topxai.cn/ai/cutout-pro)** — AI image editing and video generation platform with background removal, portrait cutout, object eraser, upscaling, colorisation and image-to-video, aggregating models such as Nano Banana Pro and Seedream with a standalone API. · [website](https://www.cutout.pro/)
- **[Deep-Image.ai](https://topxai.cn/ai/deep-image)** — AI image enhancement platform leaning toward e-commerce and print, supporting generative super-resolution, up to 4x enlargement and batches of 50 images at a time, with an API and Zapier integration. · [website](https://deep-image.ai/)
- **[Face++](https://topxai.cn/ai/face)** — Megvii's computer vision platform offering APIs and SDKs for face detection, comparison and search, liveness detection, portrait processing, OCR and image recognition, available on public cloud or offline. · [website](https://www.faceplusplus.com.cn/)
- **[GeoSpy](https://topxai.cn/ai/geospy)** — 面向执法机构与企业的图像地理定位平台，无需照片元数据，仅凭画面像素推断拍摄地点，官方称精度可达米级，现已升级为 Raven。 · [website](https://geospy.ai/)
- **[Hive Moderation](https://topxai.cn/ai/hive-moderation)** — Hive provides content understanding APIs, with models for visual, text and audio moderation, AI-generated image and video detection, OCR, and trademark or celebrity recognition, billed by usage. · [website](https://thehive.ai/)
- **[IMG2GO](https://topxai.cn/ai/img2go)** — Online image editing and conversion toolkit in the browser: 28 apps covering compression, resizing, cropping, background removal, AI retouching, colourisation and upscaling, converting between JPG, PNG, WEBP and PDF. · [website](https://www.img2go.com/)
- **[ImgLarger](https://topxai.cn/ai/imglarger)** — Online AI upscaling and enhancement site offering up to 8x enlargement with super-resolution, plus outpainting, sharpening, background removal and old photo restoration. 100 credits a month on signup. · [website](https://imglarger.com/)
- **[Ishumei](https://topxai.cn/ai/ishumei)** — Content safety and risk control platform covering text, image, audio and video moderation, plus registration and login protection, marketing risk control and LLM guardrails for live streaming, social, e-commerce and overseas businesses. · [website](https://www.ishumei.com/)
- **[Landing AI](https://topxai.cn/ai/landing-ai)** — Enterprise document processing platform: its ADE turns any document into auditable structured data with page-level coordinate citations and confidence scores, deployable to cloud or on-premise. · [website](https://landing.ai/)

- **[Let's Enhance](https://topxai.cn/ai/letsenhance)** — Online AI upscaling and enhancement tool with correction, enlargement and restoration modes, output up to 16x, 512MP and 300DPI, plus an API and an e-commerce offering called Claid.ai. · [website](https://letsenhance.io/)

- **[Pebblely](https://topxai.cn/ai/pebblely)** — E-commerce AI product image generator that turns one product photo into a range of marketing assets without Photoshop, with 100+ templates and batch generation. · [website](https://pebblely.com/)

## About

This repository is the open mirror of [topxai.cn](https://topxai.cn), turning the AI tools catalogued on the site into a list that is readable by humans and consumable by programs.

- **Chinese-first**: the [简体中文](./README.md) version is the default.
- **Auto-synced**: a GitHub Action pulls fresh data from the site API every day and regenerates this file.
- **Structured data**: the `data/` folder ships the same content as JSON and CSV.

## Data files

| File | Description |
| --- | --- |
| [`data/tools.json`](./data/tools.json) | Structured data for every tool (bilingual name & description, category, tags, official URL) |
| [`data/categories.json`](./data/categories.json) | Category definitions and grouping |
| [`data/tools.csv`](./data/tools.csv) | The same data as a spreadsheet, easy to open in Excel |
